# Disclosure, narration and epistemic asymmetry

Revision0.9 is current through V08F15; each actor has only the access route actually supplied. New retrospective October/Santa material does not enter earlier prospective states. Sixty earlier proposition identities receive a new scoped version; no older row is overwritten. V08 O### expands to LN-JP-V08-O### in the [complete reading/index](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_LN_V08_DEEP_READING.md#observation-locator-index-and-shared-field-contract), ACQUIRED-20261017-A1. Earlier OIDs keep their own volume. M=Miyagi Shiori, S=Sendai Hazuki, Maika=Utsunomiya Maika, Ami=Shirakawa Ami; Noto and Asakura have no supplied first names. P08-NNN expands to PROP-LN-JP-V08-NNN, RNNN to RULE-LN-JP-NNN, REL-V08/EPI-V08/CLM-VNN retain their LN-JP prefixes. Mainline F11 is Dec25night and precedes rewound Sunday bonus F13; retrospective evidence never becomes earlier prospective knowledge. O041/O063 correct draft piercing-date references to Sep25, distinct from Sep27 prior exit/work conversation. M0.11/S0.12 are new untested noncurrent exit candidates; eleven historical model cases preserve five failures, one exposure-qualified gift match and five baseline-tied ordinary matches, with five earlier claim forecasts separate.


Revision0.1 initialized no propositions. Revision0.2 admits V01 proposition/event states only. It does not declare either narrator globally reliable or unreliable.

Revision0.3 appends V02 disclosures and new versions of existing proposition identities, not duplicate truth stores. In V02 rows, `O###` resolves to `LN-JP-V02-O###` in the [frozen V02 index](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_LN_V02_DEEP_READING.md#observation-locator-index-and-shared-field-contract), witness ACQUIRED-20261017-A1. `P01-NNN`/`P02-NNN` expand to PROP-LN-JP-V01-NNN/PROP-LN-JP-V02-NNN. M=Miyagi Shiori, S=Sendai Hazuki, R=reader remain separate access states. V01 unversioned references retain their historical meaning; no later fact is backfilled into a prior F-state.

All records bind `LN-JP-V01 / ACQUIRED-20261017-A1` and the [frozen reading's source/hash/locator and observation contract](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_LN_V01_DEEP_READING.md#observation-locator-index-and-shared-field-contract). `O###` means `LN-JP-V01-O###`; exact native ranges and scene clocks are in that reading. M means Miyagi, S means Sendai, R means reader/analyst; these are three separate access states, never a composite omniscient character. `P001` expands to `PROP-LN-JP-V01-001`; `EPI001` to `EPI-LN-JP-V01-001`. An event row may display multiple explicitly distinguished subject transitions for the same disclosure. Latest character/relationship projections link these IDs; earlier F-states remain frozen in the reading.

## Responsibility and boundaries

This ledger owns propositions and who can know, believe, suspect, deny, conceal or misunderstand them at a specified point. Keep subject knowledge distinct from narrator access and analyst knowledge. A private narrator's account is evidence of that account, not automatic objective truth. The character ledger references information states, the relationship ledger references disclosure effects, and the claims ledger owns disputed interpretations.

Identifiers: `PROP-<witness>-<unit>-NNN` for a bounded proposition and `EPI-<witness>-<unit>-NNN` for disclosure/belief events. Do not renumber on revision. Native passage order, narrated-event time and disclosure order are separate axes. Retrospective disclosures append events and links to earlier states without rewriting earlier prospective freezes.

`KNOWS` requires a defensible access route and confidence in the proposition; asserted certainty is recorded separately as a belief if its truth is unestablished. Silence does not prove ignorance. Concealment requires evidence of knowledge and withholding; motive remains separately inferential.

## Transaction acceptance

Ensure every character claim is bounded by an accessible information route; every reader inference has its own rationale; and every narration discrepancy has a counter-reading. Freeze unresolved gaps. Use the latest justified projection only for the selected witness and time, never for all editions or all future scenes. Revision 0.1: schema only.

Revision0.2 / SHUUKURA-TX-LN-JP-V01-20261017-A1: seventeen bounded propositions, seventeen explicitly separated access-route events and eight tensions. Latest projections preserve historical reader uncertainty, character-specific knowledge, private concealment and attribution. No current fact is inferred solely from an unanswered question or from V1.

Revision0.3 / SHUUKURA-TX-LN-JP-V02-20261017-A1: twenty new subject-access events, twenty-five proposition-version rows (thirteen new identities, twelve revisions of existing identities) and thirteen new tensions. V01 versions/events remain preserved. Latest-time early September and later-read earlier June/July facts remain separate. No unspoken plan, private motive or unresolved omitted subject becomes shared knowledge by assertion.

Revision0.4 / SHUUKURA-TX-LN-JP-V03-20261017-A1: append29proposition-version rows (sixteen new identities/thirteen prior-identity revisions),22access events and15tensions. Prior source-order states remain intact. Weather discrepancy reviewed unchanged/OPEN; older parent referent still OPEN despite new report. No observer gets another narrator’s private explanation by assertion.


Revision0.5 / SHUUKURA-TX-LN-JP-V04-20261017-A1: append52proposition-version rows (18new P04 identities plus23updates to20earlier identities),26subject-access records and19asymmetry/tension records. Prior rows remain byte-identical. Unchanged historical propositions, including V02 weather discrepancy and V03 bag bluff/illness/piercing/month details, remain source-bounded; no new full witness agreement or actor knowledge is inferred from absence of later correction.


Revision0.6 / SHUUKURA-TX-LN-JP-V05-20261017-A1: append63proposition-version rows (26older IDs updated;34new IDs across37new rows),32subject-access transitions and22tensions. Old rows remain unchanged. Exact native ruby forMio/Hanamaki Kikyō verified after original journal freeze and recorded as lexical clarification, without backdating access. All body/word, event/disclosure and distinct-witness boundaries preserved; no V1/audio/web inspection claimed.


Revision0.7 / SHUUKURA-TX-LN-JP-V06-20261017-A1: append66proposition versions (34older IDs revised/32new IDs),32subject-information transitions and25tensions. Total130proposition identities. Every prior row unchanged; acquiredbookcontains589ruby elements/174unique guides, separately inspected after journal freeze with no new narrative outcome.
